DESIGN.md

show
## Concept
The player controls a tiny dot navigating a dense field of obstacles. By tapping (or clicking) they emit a short‑lived outward shockwave that pushes nearby obstacles away, briefly carving a safe corridor; a cooldown forces careful timing and strategic placement of each wave.

## Player
The avatar is a simple glowing dot that drifts through the play area. It can move in any direction (up, down, left, right, diagonally) using keyboard or touch input, and it can **emit shockwave** on demand. The verbs are *move* and *shockwave*.

## Core loop
Every few seconds new obstacles appear and drift toward the dot. The player steers to avoid collisions, watches the obstacle patterns, and decides when to trigger a shockwave to clear a path. After each wave a short cooldown prevents spamming, so the player must anticipate the next hazard and time the next wave for maximum effect. The loop repeats: navigate → evaluate → shockwave (if needed) → wait for cooldown.

## Hazards / Obstacles
- **Roaming blocks**: solid squares that travel in straight lines, bouncing off the arena edges. They form moving walls that can engulf the dot.
- **Spinning rotors**: rotating blades that sweep a fixed radius; they telegraph rotation direction before each spin.
- **Static clusters**: groups of stationary obstacles that create narrow choke points. They are immune to movement except when displaced by a shockwave.
- **Pulsing mines**: entities that expand briefly before retracting, creating temporary zones the dot must dodge.
All hazards are pushable by the shockwave but return to their original trajectories after the wave fades.

## Score
Score increases on discrete events:
- Each obstacle successfully passed without collision.
- Each successful shockwave that creates a clear corridor (i.e., after the wave the dot moves a measurable distance without hitting anything).
- Collecting occasional bonus pickups that appear in the cleared space (e.g., glowing orbs). 
The score is displayed constantly on the screen.

## Win and lose
The player loses instantly on any collision with a hazard. The game ends with a “Game Over” overlay and the final score.
The player wins by surviving a predefined number of waves (or reaching a distant exit point) without dying, triggering a “You Win!” celebration overlay.

## Feel
A frantic, high‑energy arcade pace that rewards quick decision‑making. The aesthetic is crisp neon on a dark backdrop, with sharp sound cues for each shockwave, collision, and score event. The game feels like a constant pulse of tension punctuated by satisfying bursts of clearance.

## Why it's fun
The thrill of timing a single shockwave just before an imminent collision, watching obstacles scatter and the dot slip through the newly‑opened gap, delivers that perfect blend of tension and cathartic release.

TASKS.md

show
1. HTML scaffold & meta.json - create index.html with required header, canvas, restart button, empty script block and generate meta.json containing title, description, controls, tags.
2. Game state objects - declare plain object literals for player, hazards, score, and phase with initial values at top of script.
3. Animation loop - implement startLoop using requestAnimationFrame with canceling prior loop, try/catch, clear canvas each frame.
4. Input handling - add a keys Set, bind keydown/keyup listeners, implement R-key and button restart hooks calling a reset function.
5. Player movement - apply dt-scaled physics using input: acceleration, max speed, friction, and clamp position to canvas bounds.
6. Hazards system - create hazards array, spawn hazards respecting SAFE_RADIUS, update positions with dt-scaled motion.
7. Collision detection & game over - detect player-hazard collisions, transition to "gameover" state and stop gameplay loop.
8. Score system - increase score on discrete events, render score on canvas top-left.
9. Rendering pipeline - clear canvas, draw background, hazards, player (high-contrast), and HUD each frame.
10. Game-over overlay - dim background and display "Game Over", final score, and restart instructions.
11. Observable contract - populate window.__game each frame with state, score, player position/visibility, and objective string.
12. Audio integration - lazy-initialize AudioContext in a beep() helper, invoke on hit, score, and death events.

  <style>
    body { margin: 0; background: #0a0a0f; color: #e6e6f0; font-family: ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif; }
    header, main { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; padding: 16px; }
    canvas { display: block; margin: 0 auto; background: #05050c; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; }
    .game-restart { margin-top: 10px; padding: 8px 14px; background: #1c2030; color: #7df9ff; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; cursor: pointer; }
    .game-restart:hover { background: #232842; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Shockwave Burst</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Tap to emit a short‑lived outward shockwave that pushes all nearby obstacles away, temporarily clearing a corridor for the dot. Timing is crucial due to a cooldown.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Tap / Click to release shockwave</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart to reset</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
    // === CONFIG =============================================================
    // Canvas size and context
    const W = 800, H = 600;
    const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");

    // === STATE =============================================================
    // Game state objects
    let player = { x: 400, y: 300, radius: 5 };
    let hazards = [];
    let score = 0;
    let phase = "playing";

    // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
    // Lazy-initialized AudioContext for sound effects
    let audioCtx = null;
    function beep(freq, durSec) {
      try {
        if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
        const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
        const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
        osc.type = "triangle";
        osc.frequency.value = freq;
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.04, audioCtx.currentTime);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
        osc.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
        osc.start();
        osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      } catch (e) {
        console.error("Audio error", e);
      }
    }
    // Helper functions to trigger sounds on events
    function playScoreSound() { beep(440, 0.06); }
    function playHitSound() { beep(660, 0.04); }
    function playDeathSound() { beep(110, 0.3); }
  </script>
</body>
</html>

Per-frame constants (`x += 5` every frame) make games run 2.4× faster on a 144Hz display.\n123: \n124: ```js\n125: let lastTime = performance.now();\n126: function loop(now) {\n127:   const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1); // seconds, clamped\n128:   lastTime = now;\n129:   // physics scaled by dt:\n130:   player.x += player.vx * dt;\n131:   enemy.y  += enemy.vy  * dt;\n132:   spawnTimer -= dt;\n133:   // ...\n134:   requestAnimationFrame(loop);\n135: }\n136: requestAnimationFrame(loop);\n137: ```\n138: \n139: ### 6. Restart fully resets AND cancels the prior loop\n140: \n141: Both R and the Restart button must call the same reset function. That function MUST `cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` the previous loop before scheduling a new one — otherwise loops stack on each restart and the game slows to a crawl over time.\n142: \n143: ```js\n144: let loopId = null;\n145: function startLoop() {\n146:   if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);\n147:   function tick(now) { /* ... */ loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick); }\n148:   loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);\n149: }\n150: ```\n151: \n152: ### 7. - "canvas appears blank" → the game is loading but `render()` isn't drawing anything. Check that `startLoop()` actually got called at boot, that `render()` runs every frame, and that you're issuing `ctx.fill*` / `ctx.stroke*` calls per entity each frame (not just clearing the canvas).

Common interpretations:
- "canvas appears blank" → the game is loading but `render()` isn't drawing anything. Check that `startLoop()` actually got called at boot, that `render()` runs every frame, and that you're issuing `ctx.fill*` / `ctx.stroke*` calls per entity each frame (not just clearing the canvas).
- "controls list mismatch" → the `<ul class="game-controls">` in `index.html` and the `controls` array in `meta.json` disagree. Pick one source of truth, edit the other to match exactly.
- "playtest: player.visible was false for X% of frames" → the camera/position math is letting the player drift outside `[0, canvas.width) × [0, canvas.height)`. Either clamp the player or update `player.visible` to reflect the truth (don't just hardcode `true`).
- "playtest: state took invalid value" → `window.__game.state` must be exactly `"menu"`, `"playing"`, or `"gameover"`. No other strings.
- "Required file missing: meta.json" → you didn't write the file. Use your write tool now.
- "pageerror" / "console.error" → an exception is being thrown at runtime. Read the message, locate the line, fix the bug.

**Use your write/edit tool to apply the fixes.** Do NOT respond with code in chat — the files on disk are what gets validated.

prompt (15,957 chars)
## Reference example: "asteroids"

The following is a complete, validator-passing game. **Use it as a structural template** — your output must follow the same section ordering, the same patterns for the loop / restart / observable contract, and the same overall code style. Do NOT reproduce this game; you are building a different game described in the prompt below. The example exists only to anchor structure.

Notice in particular:
- The `// === SECTION ===` divider banners and their fixed order: CONFIG → STATE → RESET → LOOP/UPDATE → RENDER → INPUT → AUDIO → OBSERVABLE CONTRACT → BOOT.
- Plain object literals for game state (no `class`).
- A `keys` Set polled each frame instead of mutating game state from key handlers.
- `if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` at the top of `startLoop()`.
- The animation loop body wrapped in `try/catch`.
- `publishState()` called once per frame at the end of the tick.
- `window.__game` populated honestly (player.visible reflects actual viewport position).
- AudioContext initialized lazily inside `beep()`, wrapped in try/catch.

### Example `meta.json`
```json
{
  "title": "Neon Asteroids",
  "description": "Rotate, thrust, and shoot. Big rocks split into small ones. Get hit and you're gone.",
  "controls": [
    "Left/Right (or A/D) to rotate",
    "Up (or W) to thrust",
    "Space to fire",
    "Press R or click Restart"
  ],
  "tags": ["arcade", "shooter", "physics"]
}
```

### Example `index.html`
```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <title>Neon Asteroids</title>
  <style>
    body { margin: 0; background: #0a0a0f; color: #e6e6f0; font-family: ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif; }
    .game-header { padding: 12px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid #1d1d2a; }
    .game-header h1 { margin: 0; font-size: 18px; color: #7df9ff; }
    .game-header .game-description { margin: 4px 0; color: #a5a5b8; }
    .game-header .game-controls { margin: 4px 0; padding-left: 18px; color: #c8c8d8; }
    main { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; padding: 16px; }
    canvas { background: #05050c; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; }
    .game-restart { margin-top: 10px; padding: 8px 14px; background: #1c2030; color: #7df9ff; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; cursor: pointer; }
    .game-restart:hover { background: #232842; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Neon Asteroids</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Rotate, thrust, and shoot. Big rocks split into small ones. Get hit and you're gone.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Left/Right (or A/D) to rotate</li>
      <li>Up (or W) to thrust</li>
      <li>Space to fire</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
  // === CONFIG =============================================================
  const W = 800, H = 600;
  const SHIP_RADIUS = 12;
  const SHIP_THRUST = 280;       // px/sec^2
  const SHIP_FRICTION = 0.6;     // velocity decay per second (1 = none)
  const SHIP_TURN = Math.PI * 1.8; // rad/sec
  const BULLET_SPEED = 520;      // px/sec
  const BULLET_LIFE = 1.0;       // sec
  const FIRE_COOLDOWN = 0.20;    // sec between shots
  const ASTEROID_BIG_R = 38;
  const ASTEROID_MID_R = 22;
  const ASTEROID_LIL_R = 12;
  const ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE = [40, 110];
  const SAFE_RADIUS_AT_SPAWN = 160; // no asteroid spawns inside this disk

  const SCORE_BIG = 20, SCORE_MID = 50, SCORE_LIL = 100;

  // === STATE ==============================================================
  const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");

  let ship;        // { x, y, vx, vy, angle, alive }
  let bullets;     // [{ x, y, vx, vy, life }]
  let asteroids;   // [{ x, y, vx, vy, r, shape: number[] }]
  let score;
  let highScore = 0;
  let phase;       // "playing" | "gameover"
  let fireCooldown;
  let keys;
  let lastTime;
  let loopId = null;
  let audioCtx = null;

  function reset() {
    ship = { x: W / 2, y: H / 2, vx: 0, vy: 0, angle: -Math.PI / 2, alive: true };
    bullets = [];
    asteroids = [];
    spawnWave(5);
    score = 0;
    phase = "playing";
    fireCooldown = 0;
    keys = new Set();
    lastTime = performance.now();
    publishState();
    startLoop();
  }

  function startLoop() {
    if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
    function tick(now) {
      try {
        const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
        lastTime = now;
        update(dt);
        render();
        publishState();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("loop error", err);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }

  function spawnWave(n) {
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) spawnAsteroid(ASTEROID_BIG_R);
  }

  function spawnAsteroid(r) {
    let x, y, tries = 0;
    do {
      x = Math.random() * W;
      y = Math.random() * H;
      tries++;
    } while (Math.hypot(x - ship.x, y - ship.y) < SAFE_RADIUS_AT_SPAWN && tries < 30);
    const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
    const speed = ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0] + Math.random() * (ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[1] - ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0]);
    asteroids.push({
      x, y,
      vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,
      vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,
      r,
      shape: makeAsteroidShape(),
    });
  }

  function makeAsteroidShape() {
    // Per-vertex radius multipliers, 8-12 vertices for a knobbly silhouette.
    const n = 8 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 5);
    const out = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) out.push(0.75 + Math.random() * 0.45);
    return out;
  }

  // === UPDATE =============================================================
  function update(dt) {
    if (phase !== "playing") return;

    // Ship.
    if (ship.alive) {
      const turnIn = (keys.has("ArrowRight") || keys.has("d") || keys.has("D") ? 1 : 0)
                   - (keys.has("ArrowLeft")  || keys.has("a") || keys.has("A") ? 1 : 0);
      ship.angle += turnIn * SHIP_TURN * dt;

      const thrusting = keys.has("ArrowUp") || keys.has("w") || keys.has("W");
      if (thrusting) {
        ship.vx += Math.cos(ship.angle) * SHIP_THRUST * dt;
        ship.vy += Math.sin(ship.angle) * SHIP_THRUST * dt;
      }
      // Mild friction so the ship doesn't drift forever.
      const decay = Math.pow(SHIP_FRICTION, dt);
      ship.vx *= decay;
      ship.vy *= decay;
      ship.x = wrap(ship.x + ship.vx * dt, W);
      ship.y = wrap(ship.y + ship.vy * dt, H);

      fireCooldown -= dt;
      if ((keys.has(" ") || keys.has("Space")) && fireCooldown <= 0) {
        fire();
        fireCooldown = FIRE_COOLDOWN;
      }
    }

    // Bullets.
    for (const b of bullets) {
      b.life -= dt;
      b.x = wrap(b.x + b.vx * dt, W);
      b.y = wrap(b.y + b.vy * dt, H);
    }
    bullets = bullets.filter((b) => b.life > 0);

    // Asteroids.
    for (const a of asteroids) {
      a.x = wrap(a.x + a.vx * dt, W);
      a.y = wrap(a.y + a.vy * dt, H);
    }

    // Bullet-asteroid collisions.
    for (let i = asteroids.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const a = asteroids[i];
      let hit = false;
      for (let j = bullets.length - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
        const b = bullets[j];
        if (Math.hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y) < a.r) {
          bullets.splice(j, 1);
          hit = true;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (hit) splitAsteroid(i);
    }

    // Ship-asteroid collisions.
    if (ship.alive) {
      for (const a of asteroids) {
        if (Math.hypot(a.x - ship.x, a.y - ship.y) < a.r + SHIP_RADIUS * 0.6) {
          ship.alive = false;
          phase = "gameover";
          if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
          beep(110, 0.3);
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    // Wave clear -> spawn next.
    if (asteroids.length === 0 && phase === "playing") {
      spawnWave(5);
    }
  }

  function fire() {
    bullets.push({
      x: ship.x + Math.cos(ship.angle) * SHIP_RADIUS,
      y: ship.y + Math.sin(ship.angle) * SHIP_RADIUS,
      vx: ship.vx + Math.cos(ship.angle) * BULLET_SPEED,
      vy: ship.vy + Math.sin(ship.angle) * BULLET_SPEED,
      life: BULLET_LIFE,
    });
    beep(820, 0.04);
  }

  function splitAsteroid(idx) {
    const a = asteroids[idx];
    asteroids.splice(idx, 1);
    if (a.r === ASTEROID_BIG_R) {
      score += SCORE_BIG;
      for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) addChild(a, ASTEROID_MID_R);
      beep(440, 0.06);
    } else if (a.r === ASTEROID_MID_R) {
      score += SCORE_MID;
      for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) addChild(a, ASTEROID_LIL_R);
      beep(560, 0.05);
    } else {
      score += SCORE_LIL;
      beep(700, 0.05);
    }
  }

  function addChild(parent, r) {
    const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
    const speed = ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0] + Math.random() * (ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[1] - ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0]);
    asteroids.push({
      x: parent.x, y: parent.y,
      vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,
      vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,
      r,
      shape: makeAsteroidShape(),
    });
  }

  // === RENDER =============================================================
  function render() {
    ctx.fillStyle = "#05050c";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

    // Asteroids.
    ctx.strokeStyle = "#a5a5b8";
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
    for (const a of asteroids) drawAsteroid(a);

    // Bullets.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#ffd23f";
    for (const b of bullets) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, 2.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }

    // Ship.
    if (ship.alive) drawShip();

    // HUD.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
    ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
    ctx.textAlign = "right";
    ctx.fillText(`Best ${highScore}`, W - 12, 22);

    if (phase === "gameover") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(5,5,12,0.65)";
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillStyle = "#ff52ae";
      ctx.fillText("Game Over", W / 2, H / 2 - 20);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
      ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, W / 2, H / 2 + 14);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#a5a5b8";
      ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", W / 2, H / 2 + 42);
    }
  }

  function drawShip() {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(ship.x, ship.y);
    ctx.rotate(ship.angle);
    ctx.strokeStyle = "#7df9ff";
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(SHIP_RADIUS, 0);
    ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.9, SHIP_RADIUS * 0.7);
    ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.5, 0);
    ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.9, -SHIP_RADIUS * 0.7);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.stroke();
    if (keys && (keys.has("ArrowUp") || keys.has("w") || keys.has("W"))) {
      ctx.strokeStyle = "#ffd23f";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.5, 0);
      ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS - 6 - Math.random() * 4, 0);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }

  function drawAsteroid(a) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    for (let i = 0; i < a.shape.length; i++) {
      const t = (i / a.shape.length) * Math.PI * 2;
      const r = a.r * a.shape[i];
      const x = a.x + Math.cos(t) * r;
      const y = a.y + Math.sin(t) * r;
      if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y); else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
    }
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.stroke();
  }

  // === INPUT ==============================================================
  document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
    if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") { reset(); return; }
    keys.add(ev.key);
    if (ev.key === " ") ev.preventDefault();
  });
  document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });
  restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);

  // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
  function beep(freq, durSec) {
    try {
      if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
      const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
      osc.type = "triangle";
      osc.frequency.value = freq;
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.04, audioCtx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
      osc.start(); osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    } catch {}
  }

  // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
  function publishState() {
    window.__game = {
      state: phase,                                  // "playing" | "gameover"
      score,
      player: {
        x: ship.x,
        y: ship.y,
        // wrap() keeps coords in [0, W) and [0, H), so visible is always true
        // — but assert it explicitly so the contract is honest.
        visible: ship.x >= 0 && ship.x < W && ship.y >= 0 && ship.y < H,
      },
      objective: "Shoot the asteroids before they hit your ship. Big rocks split into smaller ones.",
    };
  }

  // === HELPERS ============================================================
  function wrap(v, max) {
    if (v < 0) return v + max;
    if (v >= max) return v - max;
    return v;
  }

  // === BOOT ===============================================================
  reset();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

// Canvas size
const W = 800, H = 600;
const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");

// === RENDER =============================================================
// Clear canvas and render all elements each frame
function render() {
  // Clear background
  ctx.fillStyle = "#05050c";
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  // Draw hazards
  ctx.fillStyle = "#ff6666";
  hazards.forEach(h => {
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(h.x, h.y, h.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  });
  // Draw player
  ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, player.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();
  // HUD
  ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
  ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
  ctx.textAlign = "left";

}

function beep(freq, durSec) {
  try {
    if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
    const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
    osc.type = "triangle";
    osc.frequency.value = freq;
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.04, audioCtx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
    osc.start();
    osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
  } catch {}
}

// === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================

let audioCtx = null;

function beep(freq, durSec) {
  try {
    if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
    const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
    osc.type = "triangle";
    osc.frequency.value = freq;
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.04, audioCtx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
    osc.start();
    osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("Audio error", e);
  }
}

prompt (5,126 chars)
Read CONVENTIONS.md and DESIGN.md in your working directory before doing anything else.

Your job in this stage is to break the implementation into a numbered list of small, ordered tasks. The next stage will work through this list one task at a time, in a single LLM session, reloading the page after each task and capturing console errors for you to fix.

Title: Shockwave Burst
Concept: Tap to emit a short‑lived outward shockwave that pushes all nearby obstacles away, temporarily clearing a corridor for the dot; a cooldown forces careful timing.

**You must produce one output file: `TASKS.md` in the working directory.** Use your write/edit tool to create this file on disk. After your turn ends, the validator runs `readFile("TASKS.md")` — if the file does not exist on disk, validation fails and your work is wasted. **Do NOT reply with the task list as inline chat text.** The list must be written to the file.

**Tool calls MUST be emitted as actual function/tool invocations — NOT described or simulated inside your reasoning/analysis text.** Writing tool-shaped JSON like `{"filePath":"TASKS.md","content":"..."}` *inside your chain-of-thought* does NOT invoke any tool. It is just text in the analysis channel that nothing reads or executes. The same goes for `{"pattern":"**/*"}` etc. — those only do something when emitted as a real `tool_use` event from the action channel, never when written into your thinking. Several prior runs have failed by hallucinating tool calls inside reasoning; do not repeat that pattern. If at any point you "describe" a tool call in your thinking instead of invoking it, you must immediately invoke the real tool afterward. The format MUST be a numbered list, one task per line, each line starting with `<number>.`:

```
1. <Task title> — <one-sentence description of what to do>
2. <Task title> — <one-sentence description>
...
```

The next stage parses tasks by matching `^\d+\.` at the start of each line. Don't use bullet sub-lists, don't number sub-tasks (1.1, 1.2). One number, one task, one line.

### What makes a good task

- **Small enough** to complete in a single coding turn (~50–150 lines of edits).
- **Testable**: after this task, what does the page do that it didn't before?
- **Leaves the file runnable**: at the end of the task, no syntax errors, no uncaught exceptions on load. Even if the game isn't playable yet, it must load.
- **Specific**: don't say "implement player". Say "implement player movement: arrow keys set keys.left/right; in update(), apply acceleration in the pressed direction with cap at MAX_SPEED, friction otherwise; clamp x to canvas bounds."
- **Concrete numbers**: this is where pixel sizes, ms intervals, and px/sec speeds live. Pick them. The implement stage will use them verbatim.

### Required coverage (in roughly this order)

Cover at minimum, ordered as below. Add tasks if the design demands more (powerups, level progression, screen wrap, etc):

1. **HTML scaffold + meta.json**: header (h1, description, controls list), canvas, restart button per CONVENTIONS.md. Stub `<script>` block (empty). meta.json matching header text.
2. **Game state objects**: player, hazards, score, phase using plain object literals at the top of the script. Set initial values.
3. **requestAnimationFrame loop**: startLoop() with `cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` discipline at the top. Body wrapped in try/catch. Calls update(dt) and render(). Don't draw anything yet besides clearing the canvas.
4. **Input**: a `keys` Set populated by document keydown/keyup. R-key restart hook. Restart button click hook. Both call the same reset() function.
5. **Player movement**: dt-based physics tied to the key inputs from task 4. Verify each control listed in meta.json actually changes a value.
6. **Hazards**: data structure, spawn rules with a SAFE_RADIUS so nothing spawns on top of the player at t=0, motion (dt-scaled).
7. **Collision detection**: player vs hazards. On hit, transition phase to "gameover" and stop the active gameplay.
8. **Score**: discrete events that increase score, drawn on canvas at top-left.
9. **Render**: clear canvas, draw background, hazards, player, HUD. Player must be the highest-contrast object on screen.
10. **Game-over overlay**: dim background, "Game Over", final score, "Press R or click Restart".
11. **Observable contract**: populate `window.__game = { state, score, player:{x,y,visible}, objective }` every frame. `state` is "playing" | "gameover". `objective` is a 10–200 char single-sentence description.
12. **Audio**: lazy-init AudioContext inside a beep() helper, wrapped in try/catch. Hook into hit / score / death events.

You may merge two trivial tasks if they truly belong together (e.g. tasks 1+2 if state is just three lines). You may split a complex task in two. Aim for 10–15 tasks total.

**Final check before you stop**: confirm you actually called your write/edit tool with `TASKS.md` as the path. If you only described the task list in chat without invoking the tool, the file will not exist on disk and validation will fail. After the file is written, stop. Do not begin implementation.

On lose, draw \"Game Over\" + final score + restart instructions on the canvas. On win, celebrate (color burst, \"You Win!\" overlay).\n94: 4. **Restart without page reload** — both pressing R AND clicking the `<button id=\"restart\">` reset the game cleanly via the same code path. Mention both in `controls` (e.g. \"Press R or click Restart\"). On restart you MUST `cancelAnimationFrame` any pending loop before starting a new one (otherwise loops stack on each restart and the game eventually slows to a crawl).\n95: 5. **Progressive difficulty** — start gentle so the player can learn, then ramp up. Don't dump them into chaos at t=0.\n96: 6. **No dead time** — something interesting on screen at all times. Idle background animation (stars, gradient pulse, particles) is fine.\n97: 7. **Persistent score / progress UI** — drawn on the canvas at the top corner, always visible.\n98: 8. **Audio (optional but encouraged)** — generate tones with `OscillatorNode` (no external assets). **Do not auto-start audio on page load** — browsers block this and it will throw. Initialize the `AudioContext` lazily on the first keypress or click. Wrap audio code in try/catch.\n99: \n100: ## Playable Contract — non-negotiable\n101: \n102: These are not aspirations. A game that violates any of these is broken even if it runs without errors. After your implementation finishes, the validator loads the page in a real browser and runs an 8-second playtest with synthetic input — failures here are reported back to you and must be fixed before the game ships.\n103: \n104: ### 1. The player can lose\n105: \n106: There must be a collision check (or other end condition) that fires `gameOver` and stops the active gameplay loop. \"Game runs forever, no death\" is broken. Examples that have failed in the past: collision check compared a value against itself; \"spike\" collision only clamped position instead of killing the player; obstacles never reached the player.\n107: \n108: ### 2. The player can score\n109: \n110: The score must increase on a **discrete event** (a survived obstacle, a collected pickup, an enemy destroyed, a beat hit). \"Score = elapsed seconds\" is allowed but weak. Score must be drawn on the canvas at all times.\n111: \n112: ### 3. The first 3 seconds are survivable with no input\n113: \n114: Define a `SAFE_RADIUS` (e.g. 200px) around the player's starting position. When spawning hazards, **reject any spawn that is within `SAFE_RADIUS` of the player AND would reach them within 3 seconds at current hazard speed**. Pre-spawning hazards on top of the player at t=0 has caused frame-1 deaths in past games. Don't do it.\n115: \n116: ### 4. The control actually changes the outcome\n117: \n118: Whatever the player presses must, in the next frame, change a value that affects collision or score. If a control is purely cosmetic (e.g. \"stretch horizontally\" but the gaps are vertical), the game is fundamentally broken even if it runs. After writing, **re-read the game's concept and trace one frame**: does the input do what the concept claims?\n119: \n120: ### 5. dt-based physics, not per-frame constants\n121: \n122: All movement and timing must scale by delta time (seconds since last frame). Per-frame constants (`x += 5` every frame) make games run 2.4× faster on a 144Hz display.\n123: \n124: ```js\n125: let lastTime = performance.now();\n126: function loop(now) {\n127:   const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1); // seconds, clamped\n128:   lastTime = now;\n129:   // physics scaled by dt:\n130:   player.x += player.vx * dt;\n131:   enemy.y  += enemy.vy  * dt;\n132:   spawnTimer -= dt;\n133:   // ...\n134:   requestAnimationFrame(loop);\n135: }\n136: requestAnimationFrame(loop);\n137: ```\n138: \n139: ### 6. Restart fully resets AND cancels the prior loop\n140: \n141: Both R and the Restart button must call the same reset function. That function MUST `cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` the previous loop before scheduling a new one — otherwise loops stack on each restart and the game slows to a crawl over time.\n142: \n143: ```js\n144: let loopId = null;\n145: function startLoop() {\n146:   if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);\n147:   function tick(now) { /* ... */ loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick); }\n148:   loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);\n149: }\n150: ```\n151: \n152: ### 7. Game-over overlay shows score and restart instructions\n153: \n154: When the player dies, draw on the canvas: \"Game Over\", final score, current high score (if you persist one), and \"Press R or click Restart\". Do not rely on the HTML restart button alone — players need to know they can restart.\n155: \n156: ## Observable State Contract — required for playtest\n157: \n158: In addition to the rules above, your game **must** expose its current state on `window.__game` so the automated playtest can observe what the player would observe. A game that does not expose this object is rejected.\n159: \n160: ```js\n161: window.__game = {\n162:   state: \"menu\",        // \"menu\" | \"playing\" | \"gameover\" — exact strings, no others\n163:   score: 0,             // current score, number\n164:   player: {\n165:     x: 400,             // player x in canvas coordinates (0 = left)\n166:     y: 300,             // player y in canvas coordinates (0 = top)\n167:     visible: true,      // true if the player is currently rendered inside the canvas viewport\n168:   },\n169:   objective: \"Dodge falling neon blocks. Survive as long as possible.\", // 10-200 chars, one sentence\n170: };\n171: ```\n172: \n173: Rules:\n174: - The object must exist from the very first frame, before any input.\n175: - Update `state`, `score`, `player.x`, `player.y`, and `player.visible` whenever they change. The simplest approach is to update them at the end of your render/update step each frame.\n176: - `state` must only ever be one of the three exact strings. No other values.\n177: - `player.visible` is critical. **If your camera follows the player, the camera math must keep `player.visible` = true at all times.** A camera that lags the player off-screen is the most common failure here.\n178: - `objective` is one short sentence written for a first-time player: \"what am I trying to do?\" — derivable from observing the game alone, not from reading the meta.\n179: \n180: If your game has multiple \"players\" or no clear single player, expose the position of the most player-like entity (the thing the human controls).\n181: \n182: ## What the validator checks\n183: \n184: Your output is checked programmatically. No external URLs in `<script src=>`, `<link href=>`, `<img src=>` (regex scan).\n190: 5. Headless Chromium loads the page; no `console.error` or `pageerror` events in 5 seconds.\n191: 6. Canvas has non-blank pixels after 3 seconds (game is actually drawing something).\n192: 7. **Observable contract is present**: `window.__game` exists with the exact shape above, from the first frame.\n193: 8. **Playtest invariants** hold during ~8 seconds of synthetic key input (arrow keys, WASD, space):\n194:    - `state` only takes the values `\"menu\" | \"playing\" | \"gameover\"`.\n195:    - `player.visible` is true for ≥95% of polled frames.\n196:    - Either `score` changes at least once during play, or `state` reaches `\"gameover\"`.
